leaf="">这篇稿子写到一半，OpenAI 发了 GPT-6 和 Intelligent UI：问 ChatGPT 问题，它不只回文字，还会</span><span style="color:#111827;font-weight:700;"><span leaf="">在对话里直接做出图表、按钮和小工具</span></span><span leaf="">。</span></p><section style="text-align: center;" nodeleaf="" data-mpa-action-id="muytniuf319" data-pm-slice="0 0 []"><img class="rich_pages wxw-img" data-aistatus="1" data-imgfileid="100007231" data-ratio="1.152027027027027" data-s="300,640" data-src="https://mmbiz.qpic.cn/mmbiz_png/iaZPxh36Uvuz9u0svNnOMibUbxo3InBR881RKicQGTltM6NXZZPBxgXVhyOxlPA5hbzFazutLbzUbZMtWdzZmibYficBme9iadjvFIImQcFYOOuPo/640?wx_fmt=png&amp;from=appmsg" data-type="png" data-w="1184" style="border-radius: 9px;background-color: transparent;" type="block"></section><p style="margin:0 0 16px;font-size:14px;line-height:1.9;text-align:justify;"><span leaf="">我愣了一下。国庆这几天，我一直在用&nbsp;</span><span style="color:#047857;font-weight:700;"><span leaf="">Eazo</span></span><span leaf="">&nbsp;干差不多的事：不让 AI 只回文字，</span><span style="color:#111827;font-weight:700;"><span leaf="">让它直接做出能用的东西</span></span><span leaf="">。</span></p></section><section style="padding:0 12px;"><p style="margin:0 0 16px;font-size:14px;line-height:1.9;text-align:justify;"><span leaf="">起因是刷 X。时间线上最热闹的是 Muse 和 dots 这些替你办事的 Personal Agent，也有人晒用 Eazo 做的小应用：像素穿搭、快艇赛车、做霉豆腐的解压小游戏，</span><span style="color:#111827;font-weight:700;"><span leaf="">都是给自己量身做的</span></span><span leaf="">，Product Fit Me。我也下载试了试，</span><span style="color:#111827;font-weight:700;"><span leaf="">结果一天连着做了十几个</span></span><span leaf="">：给孩子的刷牙游戏、给爷爷奶奶的吃药 App、给外国朋友取中文名、声音明信片、梦的博物馆，还有一个童年的钢珠迷宫……</span></p></section><section style="padding:0 12px;"><p style="margin:0 0 16px;font-size:14px;line-height:1.9;text-align:justify;"><span leaf="">能停不下来，是因为：</span></p></section><section style="padding:0 12px;"><section style="margin:28px 0;"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="34" height="14" viewbox="0 0 34 14" style="display:block;"><rect x="0" y="0" width="4" height="4" fill="#047857"></rect><rect x="5" y="0" width="4" height="4" fill="#047857"></rect><rect x="0" y="5" width="4" height="4" fill="#047857"></rect><rect x="5" y="5" width="4" height="4" fill="#047857"></rect><rect x="0" y="10" width="4" height="4" fill="#047857"></rect><rect x="25" y="0" width="4" height="4" fill="#047857"></rect><rect x="30" y="0" width="4" height="4" fill="#047857"></rect><rect x="25" y="5" width="4" height="4" fill="#047857"></rect><rect x="30" y="5" width="4" height="4" fill="#047857"></rect><rect x="25" y="10" width="4" height="4" fill="#047857"></rect></svg><p style="margin:8px 0 0;font-family:&#39;Songti SC&#39;,&#39;Noto Serif CJK SC&#39;,&#39;Source Han Serif SC&#39;,SimSun,serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:900;color:#111827;line-height:1.8;"><span style="background:#D3F1E2;color:#111827;padding:0 2px;"><span leaf="">每一步，都是完整产品</span></span></p></section></section><section style="padding:0 12px;"><section style="margin:48px 0 24px;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:12px;"><p style="margin:0;font-size:60px;font-weight:900;font-style:italic;color:#E5E7EB;line-height:0.9;letter-spacing:-4px;flex-shrink:0;"><span leaf="">01</span></p><section style="min-width:0;"><p style="margin:0;font-size:18px;font-weight:900;color:#111827;line-height:1.35;"><span leaf="">给身边的人量身造工具</span></p><p style="margin:6px 0 0;font-family:Menlo,Consolas,monospace;font-size:9px;font-weight:700;color:#9CA3AF;line-height:1.3;letter-spacing:1.5px;"><span leaf="">Product Fit Me</span></p></section></section></section><section style="padding:0 12px;"><p style="margin:0 0 16px;font-size:14px;line-height:1.9;text-align:justify;"><span leaf="">我姐家的小朋友不爱刷牙。每天早晚都要哄，刷两下就说"刷完了"，</span><span style="color:#111827;font-weight:700;"><span leaf="">大人也不知道到底刷没刷到位</span></span></p></section><section style="padding:0 12px;"><p style="margin:0 0 16px;font-size:14px;line-height:1.9;text-align:justify;"><span leaf="">想着用Eazo做个激励他刷牙的APP，于是我想把每天 2 分钟的刷牙，</span><span style="color:#111827;font-weight:700;"><span leaf="">变成一场打怪兽的冒险</span></span></p></section><section style="padding:0 12px;"><p style="margin:0 0 16px;font-size:14px;line-height:1.9;text-align:justify;"><span leaf="">我给Eazo口喷了下我的需求，Eazo 的做法是，同一个需求先给你&nbsp;</span><span style="color:#111827;font-weight:bold;border-bottom:2px solid #F2CF5B;padding-bottom:1px;"><span leaf="">6 套以上风格截然不同的设计</span></span><span leaf="">，这些设计不是静态的UI图，而是</span><span style="color:#111827;font-weight:700;"><span leaf="">可以直接交互体验的</span></span><span leaf="">，这点做的挺有趣，主要是设计的界面都挺好看</span></p></section><section style="padding:0 12px;"><section style="margin:12px 0 22px;"><section style="display:flex;align-items:flex-start;overflow-x:scroll;-webkit-overflow-scrolling:touch;scroll-snap-type:x mandatory;"><section 16px;font-size:14px;line-height:1.9;text-align:justify;"><span leaf="">选择好风格后，它就开始进行开发了，</span><span style="color:#111827;font-weight:bold;border-bottom:2px solid #F2CF5B;padding-bottom:1px;"><span leaf="">10分钟后</span></span><span leaf="">，回来我就得到了第一版，很惊喜，</span><span style="color:#111827;font-weight:700;"><span leaf="">能直接用</span></span><span leaf="">，就是一个能直接给孩子玩的完整游戏：4
